       c=full_conv; M=c.max()

       else patience++
       if patience>... break

       grad -= grad.mean()

       candidates eta: eta_curr, 0.5, 0.25? To save time, do one step with adaptive.

Weights `make_weights(c, n)`:
We can implement two strategies and choose randomly/cycle.
Strategy 1: top-K power.
   K = max(20, int(K_frac*n)); K_frac maybe 0.02-0.2.
   top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   vals=c[top]; minv=np.min(vals); w = (vals - minv + small)**p; normalize over full length.
   p=2-4.
Strategy 2: threshold above mean.
   mean=1/(2n-1); thresh = mean * (0.5 + 0.5*(M-mean)/? ). Let's choose `thresh = max(0.5*mean, M - width)` where `width = max(0.2*(M-mean), 0.05*mean)`. Then w=max(c-thresh,0)**p. This weights top fraction. If sum small fallback top.
Strategy 3: softmax with beta= max(10, 3.0/(M-mean)). If M-mean tiny fallback.
We can use mode cycle deterministic: e.g. use threshold p=2 default; every 200 iterations use top-K; use softmax when stuck.
Maybe use full gradient from threshold weights: h = correlate(c? no weights, a). The threshold near mean weights many; gradient may push to equalize all above threshold. Good.

Need correlate function: `correlate_weights_a(w, a)` returns h_i = ∑_{k} w_k a_{k-i}. Implementation with rfft:
```
def correlate_weights_a(w, a):
    n=len(a); L=fft_size_for_corr(n) # precomputed maybe global
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), L)
    return h[:n]
```
Need ensure linear no wrap: L = 1 << ((len(w)+len(a)-1).bit_length()). For n=5200 len(w)=10399 len(a)=5200 sum-1=15598 L=16384. Good. For n variable, compute each call maybe. We can precompute in helper `get_fft_size(n)` with cache? Closures not allowed but global dict okay. But top-level state? We can use a global dictionary or attribute? Simpler compute `size = 1 << ((3*n - 1).bit_length())` each call; bit_length cheap. For conv a*a size = `1 << ((2*n -1 -1).bit_length())`? For convolution a*a length 2n-1, L >=2n-1, use `1 << ((2*n - 1 - 1).bit_length())`. For n=5200, 10399-1=10398 bit_length14 L16384. Good.
Potential issue: `np.fft.irfft` normalized by 1/N by default, so cross-correlation formula returns correct sum? DFT block DFT uses unnormalized forward and inverse 1/N, implementing circular correlation correctly. Good.
Need maybe divide by? Test small: w=[w0,w1], a=[a0,a1], L=3. ifft(W*conj(A))[0] should w0*a0 + w1*a? For i=0, we need ∑_l a_l w_l = a0w0+a1w1. Circular correlation formula with x=w,y=a: ∑_n w_n a_{n+0 mod3}=w0a0+w1a1. no wrap because L=3 and a2=0. Good.
For i=1: ∑_n w_n a_{n+1 mod3}=w0a1+w1a0 (wrap term n=2 a? zero; n=1 a2=0.) Good. So L must prevent wrap from terms where n+j<L? If L>=max len? L=3 for n=2, m=3, L=3 exactly equal m, len(w)=3? a length2. For i=1, n=2? w2? w length 3 has w2; a_{3 mod3}=a0, gives wrap w2*a0 not wanted. return c
```

Now design advanced mirror objective. Let's define `optimize_softmax(a0, time_budget, beta_style='auto')`.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_softmax(a0, time_budget, verbose=False, seed=1, use_inner=False):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    n=len(a0); Lc=next_pow2(3*n-1)
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    floor=1e-10 / max(1,n)  # 2e-14 for 5200
    a=np.maximum(a, floor); a/=a.sum()
    best=a.copy(); best_m=conv_max(a); best_score=2*n*best_m
    eta=0.03
    # weights strategy state
    style_counter=0
    patience=0
    max_patience=2000? actually iterations; no time-based.
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget:
        c=full_conv(a); M=c.max()
        if M < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m=M; best=a.copy(); patience=0
        else:
            patience+=1
        if patience>2000: break
        weights = make_weights(c,n,strategy=style_counter%3)
        grad = correlate_weights_a(weights,a)
        grad = grad + grad? factor? derivative is 2*(w*a)_i. For direction, factor irrelevant.
        grad = grad - grad.mean()
        gmax = np.abs(grad).max()
        if gmax < 1e-14: break
        grad /= gmax
        # Try step with adaptive eta
        accepted=False
        for _ in range(6):
            a_trial = a * np.exp(-eta*grad)
            a_trial = np.maximum(a_trial, floor)
            s=a_trial.sum()
            if s<1e-15: eta*=0.5; continue
            a_trial/=s
            c_t=full_conv(a_trial); M_t=c_t.max()
            if M_t < M - 1e-16:
                a=a_trial; eta=min(eta*1.02,0.5)
                accepted=True
                # update c? next loop
                break
            else:
                eta*=0.5
                if eta<1e-5: break
        if not accepted:
            # random perturbation or larger temp
            if np.random... maybe add noise
            eta=max(eta,0.005)
            # maybe mix with best
            if patience %100 ==0:
                a = interpolate 50/50 best and random param? 
        it+=1
        if verbose and it%500==0: print score
    return best
```

Let's design a continuous profile optimizer more concretely:
- Use a discrete grid length `n_profile` maybe 2048 or 4096. We can optimize the full sequence directly with smooth mirror at moderate n, which is similar to final. But low-dimensional Fourier may find better basin.
- Parameterization: p_i = normalize(base_i * (1 + eps * sin(...)))? We can use a positive exponential of a low-frequency Fourier series:

Detailed code plan:

```
import numpy as np
import time, math, sys

def next_pow2(x):
    if x <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()

def norm_sum1(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
       return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
   return a/s

def full_conv(a):
   n=len(a)
   L=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)
   c=c[:2*n-1]
   # clean small negative
   # np.maximum maybe changes sum slightly but okay
   return np.maximum(c,0.0)

def conv_max(c): return float(np.max(c))

def internal_score(a): return 2.0*len(a)*float(np.max(full_conv(a)))
```
But full_conv allocates; in optimizer use c from full_conv.

Correlate:
```
def correlate_w_a(w, a):
    n=len(a); L=next_pow2(3*n-1)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A), L)
    return h[:n]
```
Need verify w length 2n-1. If w sum1. The gradient is 2*h, but factor irrelevant. We can use h.

Make softmax weights:
```
def softmax_weights(c, beta):
   m=float(c.max())
   u=beta*(c-m)
   u=np.clip(u, -745.0, 0.0)
   z=np.exp(u)
   s=z.sum()
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 10.0: # too few? use top 50 fallback
       topk=50
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
       z=np.zeros_like(c); z[idx]=1.0
   elif s < 1e300:
       pass
   z=z/z.sum()
   return z
```
But if s finite and small, normalizing a one-hot is okay. However if z contains many zeros, z2 descent maybe fine. We can compute effective count and adjust beta externally.
Potential overflow if beta huge and c-m=0; exp(0)=1 for all max; s maybe # active. okay.

Also compute smooth max:
```
def smooth_max(c,beta):
   m=c.max()
   u=beta*(c-m)
   u=np.clip(u,-745,0)
   s=np.exp(u).sum()
   if s<=0: return m
   return m + math.log(s)/beta
```

Mirror optimizer:
```
def mirror_smooth(a0, time_budget, beta_schedule='auto', verbose=False, seed=1):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    n=len(a0)
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    floor = max(1e-12, 1e-8 / n)  # maybe 2e-12
    a=np.maximum(a, floor); a=a/a.sum()
    best=a.copy(); best_m=full_conv(best).max()
    best_score=2*n*best_m
    # initial gap
    mean_c=1.0/(2*n-1)
    gap=max(float(best_m)-mean_c, mean_c*1e-3, 1e-15)
    beta=1.0/gap
    eta=0.08
    patience=0
    max_patience=3000
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget:
       c=full_conv(a)
       M=float(c.max())
       if M < best_m - 1e-15:
           best_m=M; best=a.copy(); patience=0
       else:
           patience += 1
       if patience > max_patience:
           # reset to best with small noise
           a = add_smooth_noise(best, 0.02, seed+it)
           a=np.maximum(a, floor); a/=a.sum()
           patience=0
           # maybe lower beta
           beta *= 0.5
       # recompute gap
       gap=max(M-mean_c, 1e-15)
       # beta anneal based on elapsed fraction and patience
       frac=(time.monotonic()-t0)/time_budget
       target_beta_factor = 0.5 + 50.0*(frac**1.5)  # up to 50.5
       # But if stuck, lower factor? maintain?
       desired_beta = target_beta_factor / gap
       # gradually move beta towards desired
       beta = 0.8*beta + 0.2*desired_beta
       beta = min(max(beta, 50.0), 1e6)
       z=softmax_weights(c,beta)
       h=correlate_w_a(z,a)
       h = h - h.mean()
       hmax=np.abs(h).max()
       if hmax < 1e-15: break
       h /= hmax
       # Current smooth value
       phi_cur = smooth_max(c,beta)
       # line search
       trial_m = None
       for _ in range(8):
           a_trial = a * np.exp(-eta*h)
           a_trial = np.maximum(a_trial, floor)
           s=a_trial.sum()
           if s < 1e-15:
               eta*=0.5; continue
           a_trial = a_trial/s
           c_t=full_conv(a_trial); M_t=float(c_t.max())
           phi_t=smooth_max(c_t,beta)
           # accept if smooth improves or M improves significantly
           if phi_t < phi_cur - 1e-18 or M_t < M - 1e-16:
               a = a_trial
               if M_t < best_m - 1e-15:
                   best_m = M_t; best = a.copy()
               eta = min(eta*1.03, 0.5)
               patience=0
               break
           else:
               eta *= 0.5
               if eta < 1e-4:
                   break
       else:
           # no improvement: small random kick or mix with best
           if patience % 25 == 0:
               # mix 10% random noise
               a = norm_sum1(0.9*a + 0.1*smooth_random_seq(n, seed+it))
               a = np.maximum(a, floor); a/=a.sum()
           if patience > 500:
               a = best.copy()
               # increase beta? decrease?
               eta = 0.05
           max_patience = max(500, max_patience//2?) no, keep.
       it +=1
       if verbose and (it % 500 ==0): print...
    # final polish maybe top-k? 
    return best, best_score
```

We need `smooth_random_seq(n, seed)` top-level:
- Use rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
- Create smooth positive vector: combine random low-frequency sine/cos.
```
def smooth_random_seq(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m=min(64, max(4, n//64))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.zeros(n)
    modes=rng.choice(np.arange(1,65), size=min(m,64), replace=False)
    for k in modes:
       amp=rng.uniform(0.1,1.0)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
       out += amp*np.sin(2*math.pi*k*x + phase)
    out = np.exp(0.1*out)  # keep positive smooth
    return norm_sum1(out)
```
Loop up to64, n=5200, okay if few. Could use FFT random faster:
```
L=next_pow2(n)
F=np.zeros(L//2+1, dtype=complex)
freqs=np.arange(1, min(L//4,len?)+1)
F[freqs]=rng.normal(size=len(freqs)) + 1j*rng.normal(size=len(freqs))
noise=np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:n]
out=np.exp(0.1*(noise / (np.std(noise)+1e-12)))
```

def make_profiles(n):

   profiles=[]
   profiles.append(np.ones(n))
   profiles.append(x)
   profiles.append(1-x)
   profiles.append((x+1e-6)**0.5?) etc.

def local_coord(a, time_budget, seed):
   n=len(a); a=norm_sum(a); best=...; M=...
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   while time < budget:
      c=full_conv(a); top_lags=argpartition top 20

      for lag in top_lags[:10]:
         # contributions to this lag

         idx=np.arange(js,je+1)
         contrib=a[idx]*a[lag-idx]

1. `clamp_normalize(a, floor=1e-12)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
a=np.maximum(a, floor)
s=a.sum()
if s<=0: return np.ones_like(a)*floor
return a/s
```
But if input has inf, nan_to_num. For final sequence, we want no inf. Good.

2. `safe_eval(a)`:
```
try:
   a_arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   if a_arr.size==0: return inf
   a_list=[float(x) for x in a_arr]
   return float(evaluate_sequence(a_list))
except Exception: return inf
```
This calls global evaluate_sequence. Use for final and maybe parent.

3. `eval_internal(a)`:
```
c=full_conv(a); return 2.0*len(a)*float(c.max())
```
But if not normalized, objective scale invariant but our internal assumes sum may not1? If a not normalized, max c scales sum^2; objective formula =2n*max_c/(sum)^2. For arbitrary a, should compute with sum. But we always normalize before internal. For interpolation, maybe. Define `objective_internal(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray; sum; if sum<1e-15 return inf; c=full_conv(a); return 2*n*c.max()/(sum*sum)
```
But performance for random profiles sum≈. Use normalized. To be safe, use normalized in helpers: `norm_sum1` first. We can compute `2*n*c.max()` for normalized. If a not normalized due to floor? We normalize. Use `ensure_normalized` in callers.

4. `interpolate_sequence(f, new_n)`:
- f normalized nonnegative.
- `old_n=len(f); if old_n<2: ...`
- Use `np.interp` with x_old = np.linspace(0,1,old_n). But for sequence endpoints maybe not periodic. New_n. Clip negative, normalize.
Could also use `np.convolve`? no.

5. `random_smooth_seq(n, seed)`:
```
rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
# use FFT for smooth noise
L=next_pow2(n)
F=np.zeros((L//2+1), dtype=np.complex128)
limit=max(2, L//16)
for k in range(1, limit):
    F[k] = rng.normal() + 1j*rng.normal()
noise=np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:n]
sd=noise.std()
if sd>1e-15: noise=noise/sd
out=np.exp(0.2*noise)
return norm_sum1(out)
```
This creates strict positive smooth random. If seed changes, okay. F loop up to L/16 e.g. 1024, okay. But for many calls, loop cost. Limit L//64 maybe. Use random normal vector and FFT? We can create `F = (rng.normal(size=L//2+1)+1j*rng.normal(...))`, zero high frequencies. This is faster:
```
F=np.zeros(L//2+1, complex)
low = max(2, L//32)
F[1:low]=rng.normal(size=low-1)+1j*rng.normal(size=low-1)
noise=np.fft.irfft(F,L)[:n]
```
For L=16384 low512, okay. Then out=exp(0.2*(noise/sd)), normalize.

6. `parametric_sequence(n, seed)` maybe if no parent:
- Try a set and choose min internal with short mirror? Not necessary if parent present. But fallback should be decent. We can just use `norm_sum1(np.sin(np.pi*x)**2)`? Uniform score2; but parent missing rare. Use linear decreasing optimized α maybe:
```
x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
v=1 - 0.65*x
v=np.maximum(v,1e-6)
```
Score continuous H~0.93 objective~1.86, okay. Or maybe 3-block? Use simple.

7. `weights_and_phi(c,beta,min_eff=10, max_iter=5)` top-level.
- Need top fallback. Use `np.argpartition(c, -k)` when needed. If c all zeros? not.
```
def weights_and_phi(c, beta, min_eff=10.0):
    nlen=len(c)
    m=float(c.max())
    beta_use=beta
    for _ in range(6):
        u=beta_use*(c-m)
        u=np.clip(u, -745.0, 0.0)
        z=np.exp(u)
        s=float(z.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s) and s >= min_eff:
            break
        beta_use *= 0.35
        if beta_use < 10.0:
            break
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-100:
        k=50
        idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
        z=np.zeros_like(c)
        z[idx]=1.0
        s=k
        beta_use=1.0
    w=z/s
    if beta_use <=0: phi=m
    else: phi=m + math.log(s)/beta_use
    return w, phi, beta_use
```
But if we reduced beta_use too much and s large (maybe uniform), okay. Note if `s` from last loop. If break due to beta<10 but s still <min_eff, weights maybe top few with low beta? Actually if beta<10 and c scale1e-4, u small, zsum~10000 (uniform). Good.

8. `gradient_from_weights(w,a)` as correlate.

9. `mirror_smooth(a0, time_budget, beta_start=1.0, beta_end=25.0, stall_s=60.0, sigma=0.03, seed=1, verbose=False)`:
Let's code with care.
```
def mirror_smooth(a0, time_budget, beta_start=1.0, beta_end=25.0, stall_s=60.0, noise_amp=0.03, seed=1, verbose=False):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if time_budget <=0: return norm_sum1(a0)
    a=norm_sum1(a0)
    n=len(a)
    floor=1e-10
    a=np.maximum(a,floor); a=a/a.sum()
    best=a.copy()
    c_best=full_conv(best); best_m=float(c_best.max()); best_score=2*n*best_m
    last_improve=t0
    eta=0.10
    beta_current=beta_start / max( (best_m - 1.0/(2*n-1)), 1e-15)
    # clamp
    beta_current = min(max(beta_current, 50.0), 200000.0)
    it=0
    verbose_iter=500
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_budget:
        c=full_conv(a); M=float(c.max())
        score=2*n*M
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score=score; best_m=M; best=a.copy(); last_improve=time.monotonic(); eta=min(eta*1.02,0.4)
        gap=max(M - 1.0/(2*n-1), 1e-15)
        elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
        frac=min(1.0, elapsed/max(time_budget,1e-9))
        target_factor = beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*(frac**1.0)
        if elapsed - last_improve > stall_s:
            target_factor = max(beta_start*0.5, target_factor*0.7)
        beta_target = target_factor / gap
        beta_target = min(max(beta_target, 50.0), 200000.0)
        beta_current = 0.6*beta_current + 0.4*beta_target
        w,phi_cur,beta_eff = weights_and_phi(c, beta_current, min_eff=10.0)
        h=gradient_from_weights(w,a)
        h[h < -1e-14?] no
        h = h - h.mean()
        hmax=float(np.abs(h).max())
        if hmax < 1e-15:
            break
        h /= hmax
        # line search
        accepted=False
        # Try up to 5 reductions
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            step=eta*mult
            a_trial = a * np.exp(-step*h)
            a_trial = np.maximum(a_trial, floor)
            s=a_trial.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                continue
            a_trial = a_trial/s
            c_t=full_conv(a_trial); M_t=float(c_t.max())
            phi_t,_,_ = weights_and_phi(c_t, beta_eff, min_eff=10.0)  # use same beta
            if phi_t < phi_cur - 1e-18 or M_t < M - 1e-17:
                a=a_trial; accepted=True
                if M_t < best_m - 1e-17:
                    best_m=M_t; best=a.copy(); best_score=2*n*M_t; last_improve=time.monotonic()
                break
        if accepted:
            eta = min(eta*1.03, 0.5)
        else:
            eta = max(eta*0.6, 1e-4)
            if time.monotonic() - last_improve > stall_s:
                # reset with noise around best
                amp = noise_amp * max(1.0, elapsed/ max(time_budget,1.0))
                base = random_smooth_seq(n, seed+it)
                a = (1.0-amp)*best + amp*base
                a = clamp_normalize(a, floor)
                eta=0.15
                # also reset beta to start?
                beta_current = beta_start / max(best_m - 1.0/(2*n-1), 1e-15)
                last_improve=time.monotonic()
        it +=1
        if verbose and it % verbose_iter ==0:
            print(f"    mirror n={n} it={it} score={best_score:.7f} current={2*n*M:.7f} eta={eta:.4f} beta={beta_eff:.0f}", flush=True)
    return best
```
Issues:
- `weights_and_phi(c_t, beta_eff)` recomputes zsum with beta_eff; if beta_eff was reduced for current c due to min_eff? Fine. But phi_t uses min_eff may reduce beta further; okay. The phase of phi may differ if weights adjust due to min_eff? Accept criteria maybe inconsistent if beta reduced for current due to zsum<min_eff but not for trial? It uses same function, okay.
- If no accept and stall, we set `last_improve` to now, causing repeated resets? But a changed; maybe need only reset and then allow progress. If no progress immediately after reset, last_improve now prevents another for stall_s. Good.
- If `eta` grows to0.5, exponent max0.5 (30% change) okay. If eta too small 1e-4, progress none.
- Patience based on best score not current; if current improves but best not, phi may decrease but no best. It may continue.
- Could loop too many full_conv; if 5200, maybe 10k iterations. Fine.

But there is a serious issue: The mirror descent step `a * exp(-step*h)` with h normalized by max. The relation to step in quotient: If h has mean subtracted and max1, exponent range ≤2. Step up to0.5 large. Good. However the sign: We want reduce high weighted c. h_i = derivative/2. If h_i positive (coordinate contributes to active peaks), exp(-step*h) reduces a_i; if negative, increases. Good.

Can we use a multiplicative update that is a Bregman projection onto smooth objective? Good.

Potential issue with full softmax: If weights concentrate top coefficients, the gradient h_i may be small for many coords; normalized by max makes tiny coordinates huge relative? It may move mass strongly from low h to high h. Should be okay.

Maybe we should include a term to avoid reducing all active peaks at expense of creating new peaks: Softmax includes all coefficients above threshold if beta moderate. Good.

After main mirror, maybe a high-beta final polish:
- Run `mirror_smooth` again with beta_start=10, beta_end=100, time_budget final, noise_amp small (0.01). Use current best. This focuses max.

But our mirror_smooth beta_end=25, cap. For final, beta_end=100.

Now, length exploration. Starting n maybe 5200. We can try new length 6500/8000. However optimizing each length separately takes time. Time budget: if we spend on main at 5200 for e.g. 800s, length exploration 2*200s, final 300s. Total 1600. Good.

But if a longer length yields significantly better, optimizing 200s may suffice? Maybe. Parent at 5200 score1.504; longer maybe 1.503. We can explore.

Let's test correlation formula with weights linear by small mental for n=2. Suppose a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. gradient_from_weights: L=next_pow2(3*2-1=5)=8. W padded length8, A [a0,a1,0...]. Ifft(W*conj(A))[0]=? W0a0+W1a1? Circular: ∑_l W_l A_{l} (mod) for l=0 W0a0, l=1 W1a1, l=2 W2a? A2=0, no wrap? But L=8, l=6 A_{6}=0, l=7 0. Good. h0=a0w0+a1w1. In circular corr ∑_l W_l A_{l+1}: l=1 gives W1 A2=0, not W2 A1. I may have formula reversed! Let's re-evaluate. We need h_i = ∑_{l=0}^{n-1} a_l w_{i+l}. For i=1: l=0 a0 w1, l=1 a1 w2. So h1=a0 w1 + a1 w2. The formula ifft(?): We want ∑_l a_l w_{i+l}. This is correlation of ? Let's define x=a (length n), y=w (length m). Circular cross-correlation r_i = ∑_l x_l y_{l+i} = ∑ a_l w_{i+l}. This is ifft(FFT(x) * conj(FFT(y)))? General property: r_{xy}[i]=∑_n x[n] y[n+i] = ifft(X * conj(Y)). Yes. Here x=a, y=w. So use A*conj(W), not W*conj(A)! Earlier I swapped. Test: A*conj(W) ifft gives ∑ a_l w_{l+i}. Good. For i=1: l=0 a0w1, l=1 a1w2. Great. So `h = irfft(A * np.conj(W), L)`. Need L >= m+n? We used same. Good. Update gradient function to A*conj(W). Let's verify i=0: a0w0+a1w1. derivative weighted c0/c1. Good.
Thus `gradient_from_weights(w,a)`:
```
A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); h=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(W), L)[:n]
```
This computes ∑ a_l w_{l+i}. Good. Need padding L ≥ m+n-1? For i up to n-1, max l+i = n-1 + n-1 =2n-2 = m-1, okay if no wrap. Dangerous wrap if l+i-L < n? For L > max index 2n-2. To avoid, need L > max_l (m-1?) Actually using A * conj(W), circular sum l=0..L-1 x_l y_{l+i modL}. x nonzero l=0..n-1. If l+i >= L, wrap to l+i-L. We need no l in 0..n-1 with l+i >=L and l+i-L < n? def fft_size_n(n): return next_pow2(2*n-1)
def conv_and_fft(a):
   L=fft_size_n(len(a)); A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L)[:2n-1]; return np.maximum(c,0), A
def gradient_from_weights_with_fft(w,a_fft,L):
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); h=np.fft.irfft(a_fft*np.conj(W),L)[:len(a)]
   return h
```
This saves one rfft per iteration (conv already computed A). Good. But if we also do trial full_conv, it computes A. Fine.

Now, `smooth_max_fixed` implementation:
```
def smooth_max_fixed(c,beta):
   m=float(c.max())
   if beta <= 1e-12: return m
   u=beta*(c-m)
   u=np.clip(u,-745,0)
   s=float(np.exp(u).sum())
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return m
   return m + math.log(s)/beta
```
For beta_eff maybe float large; `u` for far below may underflow to 0, sum at least1. Good.

Let's refine parametric fallback to be decent. We can try a simple linear family and use quick mirror? If parent missing, we have full time; we can construct a random sequence? But parent likely. Implement:
```
def fallback_sequence(n=5200):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   candidates=[]
   for beta in [0.0,0.3,0.6,0.9,1.2]:
      v=np.maximum(1.0 - beta*x, 1e-6)
      candidates.append(v)
   for alpha in [-0.8,-0.4,0.4,0.8]:
      v=np.maximum(1.0 + alpha*np.sin(2*math.pi*x),1e-6)
      candidates.append(v)
   best=None; bestsc=inf
   for v in candidates:
      a=norm_sum1(v); sc=current_score(a)
      if sc<bestsc: bestsc=sc; best=a
   return best,bestsc
```
But `current_score` uses FFT, O(n^2) for maybe n=5200 and 10 candidates okay. If no parent, we can also use mirror to improve. But parent expected.

```
def random_smooth_seq(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    L=fft_size_n(n)  # 2n-1 pow2
    F=np.zeros(L//2+1, dtype=np.complex128)
    low=max(2, L//64)
    if low < L//2+1:
       f1=rng.normal(size=low-1)
       f2=rng.normal(size=low-1)
       F[1:low]=f1+1j*f2
    noise=np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:n]
    sd=float(np.std(noise))
    if sd < 1e-15:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    noise=noise/sd
    out=np.exp(0.2*noise)
    return norm_sum1(out)
```
Could `L=fft_size_n(n)=next_pow2(2n-1)`, for n=5200 L16384; low256. Good.

def conv_main(a, L):
   n=len(a)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:2*n-1]
   return np.maximum(c,0.0), A
```

Implement `gradient_from_weights_fft`:
```
def gradient_from_weights_fft(w, a_fft, L, n):
    W=np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(a_fft * np.conj(W), L)
    return h[:n]
```
Good.

`smooth_max_fixed`:
```
def smooth_max_fixed(c,beta):
   m=float(c.max())
   if beta <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(beta): return m
   u=beta*(c-m)
   u=np.clip(u,-745,0)
   s=float(np.exp(u).sum())
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return m
   return m + math.log(s)/beta
```

Now, `interpolate_sequence`:
```
def interpolate_sequence(f,new_n):
   f=np.asarray(f,float)
   old_n=len(f)
   if old_n == new_n: return norm_sum1(f)
   if old_n <=1: return np.ones(new_n)/new_n
   f=norm_sum1(f)
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
   y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,f)
   y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
   s=y.sum()
   if s<1e-15: y=np.ones(new_n)/new_n
   else: y=y/s
   return y
```
If new_n<1? no.

`fallback_sequence`: use `current_score`. Need if `n` not 1? Use default 5200.
```
def fallback_sequence(n=5200):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   best=None; best_sc=inf
   for beta in [0.0,0.3,0.6,0.9,1.2,1.5]:
      v=np.maximum(1.0 - beta*x, 1e-6)
      a=norm_sum1(v); sc=current_score(a)
      if sc<best_sc: ...
   for alpha in [-0.8,-0.4,-0.15,0.15,0.4,0.8]:
      v=np.maximum(1.0 + alpha*np.sin(2*math.pi*x),1e-6)
      ...
   if best is None: best=norm_sum1(np.ones(n)); sc=current_score(best)
   return best, best_sc
```

def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    time_budget=max(120.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)
    hard_end=t_start + time_budget
    best=None; best_score=inf
    # parent
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       if p.ndim==1 and p.size>0:
          p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
          p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
          if p.sum() > 1e-8:
             best=norm_sum1(p)
             best_score=current_score(best)
             print("Parent n=%d score=%.7f"%(len(best),best_score), flush=True)
    except Exception:
       pass
    if best is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
       n_f=int(min(MAX_LEN_LIMIT, 5200))
       best,best_score=fallback_sequence(n_f)
       print("Fallback n=%d score=%.7f"%(len(best),best_score), flush=True)
    pool=[]
    add_to_pool(pool,best_score,best)
    orig_n=int(len(best))
    # raw length scan
    base_n=orig_n
    offsets=[500,1000,2000,3500,5000,8000]
    candidate_lens=[base_n]
    for off in offsets:
       nl=base_n+off

    for mul in [1.15, 1.35]:
       nl=int(base_n*mul)
       if nl>base_n and nl<=MAX_LEN_LIMIT: candidate_lens.append(nl)
    # unique within 200
    unique_lens=[]
    for vl in candidate_lens:
       if vl<=0 or vl>MAX_LEN_LIMIT: continue
       dup=False
       for uu in unique_lens:
          if abs(vl-uu)<=200: dup=True; break

    unique_lens.sort()

       if rem < 5: break

       cand=interpolate_sequence(best, nl)
       sc=current_score(cand)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
          best=cand; best_score=sc; add_to_pool(pool,best,best_score?)
```

```
def mirror_smooth(...):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if time_budget <= 0.05:
        a=clip_normalize(a0,1e-12)
        return a, current_score(a)
    a=clip_normalize(a0,1e-10)
    n=len(a)
    if n < 2: ...
    L=fft_size_n(n)
    best=a.copy()
    c,_ = conv_main(best,L)
    best_m=float(np.max(c)); best_score=2.0*n*best_m
    mean_c=1.0/(2*n-1)
    gap=max(best_m-mean_c, 1e-15)
    beta_current = beta_start / gap
    beta_current = min(max(beta_current, 50.0), 200000.0)
    eta=0.12
    fail_count=0
    stall_count=0
    last_progress=t0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_budget:
       c,A=conv_main(a,L)
       M=float(np.max(c))
       score=2.0*n*M
       if score < best_score - 1e-13:
           best_score=score; best_m=M; best=a.copy(); last_progress=time.monotonic(); stall_count=0; fail_count=0
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
       frac=min(1.0, elapsed/max(time_budget,1e-9))
       target_factor=beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*frac
       if elapsed - last_progress > stall_s:
           target_factor = max(beta_start*0.4, target_factor*0.6)
       gap=max(M - mean_c, 1e-15)
       beta_target=target_factor / gap
       beta_target=min(max(beta_target, 50.0), 300000.0)
       beta_current = 0.6*beta_current + 0.4*beta_target
       if not np.isfinite(beta_current) or beta_current<=0: beta_current=beta_start/gap
       w, phi_cur, beta_eff = weights_and_phi(c, beta_current)
       h=gradient_from_weights_fft(w,A,L,n)
       h = h - h.mean()
       hmax=float(np.abs(h).max())
       if hmax < 1e-15:
           break
       h /= hmax
       accepted=False
       empol? for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125):
           step=eta*mult
           a_trial = a * np.exp(-step*h)
           a_trial = np.maximum(a_trial, 1e-10)
           s=float(a_trial.sum())
           if s < 1e-15: continue
           a_trial /= s
           c_t = conv_trial(a_trial,L)
           M_t=float(np.max(c_t))
           phi_t=smooth_max_fixed(c_t, beta_eff)
           if phi_t < phi_cur - 1e-16 or M_t < M - 1e-17:
               a=a_trial; accepted=True
               if M_t < best_m - 1e-17:
                   best_m=M_t; best=a.copy(); best_score=2*n*M_t; last_progress=time.monotonic(); stall_count=0; fail_count=0
               if mult >=0.99:
                   eta=min(eta*1.03,0.5)
               else:
                   eta=max(eta*mult,1e-4)
               last_progress=time.monotonic()
               fail_count=0
               break
       if not accepted:
           eta=max(eta*0.55,1e-4)
           fail_count +=1
           if fail_count >= 120 and time.monotonic()-last_progress > max(5.0, stall_s*0.25):
               base=random_smooth_seq(n, seed + 1000*fail_count + it)
               a=(0.98*a + 0.02*base)
               a=clip_normalize(a,1e-10)
               eta=max(eta,0.06)
               fail_count=0
       if time.monotonic() - last_progress > stall_s:
           amp=min(0.45, noise_amp*(1.0+0.3*stall_count))
           base=random_smooth_seq(n, seed + 7777 + 10001*stall_count + it)
           a=(1-amp)*best + amp*base
           a=clip_normalize(a,1e-10)
           eta=0.2
           beta_current = beta_start / max(best_m-mean_c,1e-15)
           beta_current=min(max(beta_current,50.0),200000.0)
           stall_count +=1
           fail_count=0
           last_progress=time.monotonic()
       it +=1
       if verbose and it % 400 ==0:
           print(...)
    # Ensure returned best normalized
    best=clip_normalize(best,1e-12)
    # recompute score? maybe internal
    sc=current_score(best)
    return best, sc
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

The solver keeps sequences normalized to sum 1 and uses:
1. FFT-based evaluation of linear convolution.
2. Softmax-smoothed multiplicative gradient descent on the simplex.
3. Adaptive temperature (softmax beta) and line-search step size.
4. Smooth random restarts and length interpolation.
5. Final exact validation with evaluate_sequence when available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math
import operator


MAX_LEN_LIMIT = 9000


def next_pow2(x):
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def fft_size_n(n):
    return next_pow2(2 * n - 1)


def norm_sum1(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or arr.size == 0:
        if arr.size == 0:
            return np.ones(1)
        return np.ones_like(arr) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def clip_normalize(a, floor=0.0):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        arr = np.maximum(arr, float(floor))
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-300 or arr.size == 0:
        if arr.size == 0:
            return np.ones(1)
        return np.ones_like(arr) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def conv_main(a, L):
    n = len(a)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0), A


def conv_trial(a, L):
    n = len(a)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def current_score(a):
    a = norm_sum1(a)
    n = len(a)
    L = fft_size_n(n)
    c, _ = conv_main(a, L)
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def safe_eval(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        lst = arr.tolist()
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    old_n = len(f)
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return norm_sum1(f)
    if old_n <= 1:
        return np.ones(new_n) / float(new_n)
    f = norm_sum1(f)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(new_n) / float(new_n)
    return y / s


def random_smooth_seq(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    L = fft_size_n(n)
    F = np.zeros(L // 2 + 1, dtype=np.complex128)
    low = max(2, L // 64)
    low = min(low, L // 2 + 1)
    if low > 1:
        r1 = rng.normal(size=low - 1)
        r2 = rng.normal(size=low - 1)
        F[1:low] = r1 + 1j * r2
    noise = np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:n]
    sd = float(np.std(noise))
    if sd < 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n) / float(n)
    noise = noise / sd
    out = np.exp(0.20 * noise)
    return clip_normalize(out, 1e-12)


def fallback_sequence(n=5200):
    n = int(max(100, min(MAX_LEN_LIMIT, n)))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    best = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    for beta in (0.0, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.2, 1.5):
        v = np.maximum(1.0 - beta * x, 1e-6)
        a = norm_sum1(v)
        sc = current_score(a)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a

    for alpha in (-0.8, -0.4, -0.15, 0.15, 0.4, 0.8):
        v = np.maximum(1.0 + alpha * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * x), 1e-6)
        a = norm_sum1(v)
        sc = current_score(a)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a

    for p in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
        v = np.maximum(np.sin(math.pi * x), 1e-6) ** p
        a = norm_sum1(v)
        sc = current_score(a)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a

    if best is None:
        best = norm_sum1(np.ones(n))
        best_sc = current_score(best)
    return best, best_sc


def weights_and_phi(c, beta, min_eff=10.0):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    beta_use = float(beta)
    if beta_use <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(beta_use):
        beta_use = 1.0

    z = None
    s = 0.0
    for _ in range(10):
        u = beta_use * (c - m)
        u = np.clip(u, -745.0, 0.0)
        z = np.exp(u)
        s = float(np.sum(z))
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            ss = float(np.dot(z, z))
            eff = (s * s) / ss if ss > 0.0 else 0.0
        else:
            eff = 0.0
        if eff >= min_eff:
            break
        beta_use *= 0.4
        if beta_use < 1.0:
            break

    if z is None or s <= 1e-300:
        k = min(50, len(c))
        if k <= 0:
            z = np.ones_like(c)
            s = float(len(c))
            beta_use = 1.0
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
            z = np.zeros_like(c)
            z[idx] = 1.0
            s = float(len(idx))
            beta_use = 1.0

    w = z / s
    if beta_use > 0.0 and s > 0.0:
        phi = m + math.log(s) / beta_use
    else:
        phi = m
    return w, phi, beta_use


def smooth_max_fixed(c, beta):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        return m
    u = beta * (c - m)
    u = np.clip(u, -745.0, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(u)))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return m
    return m + math.log(s) / beta


def gradient_from_weights_fft(w, a_fft, L, n):
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(a_fft * np.conj(W), L)
    return h[:n]


def add_to_pool(pool, score, arr, max_pool=5):
    if arr is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    pool.append([float(score), np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()])
    pool.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    while len(pool) > int(max_pool):
        pool.pop()


def mirror_smooth(
    a0,
    time_budget,
    beta_start=1.0,
    beta_end=25.0,
    stall_s=60.0,
    noise_amp=0.03,
    seed=1,
    verbose=False,
):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    time_budget = float(max(0.0, time_budget))

    a = clip_normalize(a0, 1e-10)
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return a, float("inf")
    if time_budget <= 0.05:
        return a, current_score(a)

    L = fft_size_n(n)
    best = a.copy()
    c_best, _ = conv_main(best, L)
    best_m = float(np.max(c_best))
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_m)
    mean_c = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)

    gap = max(best_m - mean_c, 1e-15)
    beta_current = beta_start / gap
    beta_current = min(max(beta_current, 50.0), 200000.0)

    eta = 0.12
    fail_count = 0
    stall_count = 0
    last_progress = t0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_budget:
        c, A = conv_main(a, L)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        score = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if score < best_score - 1e-13:
            best_score = score
            best_m = M
            best = a.copy()
            last_progress = time.monotonic()
            stall_count = 0
            fail_count = 0

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(time_budget, 1e-9))
        target_factor = beta_start + (beta_end - beta_start) * frac
        if elapsed - last_progress > stall_s:
            target_factor = max(beta_start * 0.4, target_factor * 0.6)

        gap = max(M - mean_c, 1e-15)
        beta_target = target_factor / gap
        beta_target = min(max(beta_target, 50.0), 300000.0)
        beta_current = 0.6 * beta_current + 0.4 * beta_target
        if not np.isfinite(beta_current) or beta_current <= 0.0:
            beta_current = beta_start / gap

        w, phi_cur, beta_eff = weights_and_phi(c, beta_current, min_eff=10.0)
        if beta_eff < beta_current * 0.2:
            beta_current = beta_eff

        h = gradient_from_weights_fft(w, A, L, n)
        h = h - h.mean()
        hmax = float(np.abs(h).max())
        if hmax < 1e-15:
            break
        h = h / hmax

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            step = eta * mult
            a_trial = a * np.exp(-step * h)
            a_trial = np.maximum(a_trial, 1e-10)
            s_trial = float(a_trial.sum())
            if s_trial < 1e-15:
                continue
            a_trial = a_trial / s_trial

            c_t = conv_trial(a_trial, L)
            M_t = float(np.max(c_t))
            phi_t = smooth_max_fixed(c_t, beta_eff)

            if phi_t < phi_cur - 1e-16 or M_t < M - 1e-17:
                a = a_trial
                accepted = True
                if M_t < best_m - 1e-17:
                    best_m = M_t
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_m)
                    last_progress = time.monotonic()
                    stall_count = 0
                    fail_count = 0
                if mult >= 0.99:
                    eta = min(eta * 1.03, 0.5)
                else:
                    eta = max(eta * mult, 1e-4)
                last_progress = time.monotonic()
                fail_count = 0
                break

        if not accepted:
            eta = max(eta * 0.55, 1e-4)
            fail_count += 1
            if fail_count >= 120 and time.monotonic() - last_progress > max(5.0, 0.25 * stall_s):
                base = random_smooth_seq(n, int(seed) + 1000 * fail_count + it)
                a = clip_normalize(0.98 * a + 0.02 * base, 1e-10)
                eta = max(eta, 0.06)
                fail_count = 0

        if time.monotonic() - last_progress > stall_s:
            amp = min(0.45, float(noise_amp) * (1.0 + 0.30 * stall_count))
            base = random_smooth_seq(n, int(seed) + 7777 + 10001 * stall_count + it)
            a = clip_normalize((1.0 - amp) * best + amp * base, 1e-10)
            eta = 0.20
            beta_current = beta_start / max(best_m - mean_c, 1e-15)
            beta_current = min(max(beta_current, 50.0), 200000.0)
            stall_count += 1
            fail_count = 0
            last_progress = time.monotonic()

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 400 == 0:
            print(
                "    mirror n={} it=%d best=%.7f cur=%.7f eta=%.4f beta=%.0f".format(
                    n, it, best_score, score, eta, beta_eff
                ),
                flush=True,
            )

    best = clip_normalize(best, 1e-12)
    sc = current_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = best_score
    return best, sc


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(max(300.0, budget_s))
    hard_end = t_start + max(120.0, total_budget - 60.0)

    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 1e-8:
                best = norm_sum1(p)
                if len(best) > MAX_LEN_LIMIT:
                    best = interpolate_sequence(best, MAX_LEN_LIMIT)
                best_score = current_score(best)
                print(f"Parent n={len(best)} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)
    except Exception:
        best = None

    if best is None or not np.isfinite(best_score):
        n_f = int(min(MAX_LEN_LIMIT, 5200))
        best, best_score = fallback_sequence(n_f)
        print(f"Fallback n={len(best)} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    pool = []
    add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best, 6)
    base_n = int(len(best))

    # Cheap length scan via interpolation.
    offsets = (500, 1000, 2000, 3500, 5000, 8000)
    cand_lens = [base_n]
    for off in offsets:
        nl = base_n + off
        if nl > base_n and nl <= MAX_LEN_LIMIT:
            cand_lens.append(nl)
    for mul in (1.15, 1.35):
        nl = int(base_n * mul)
        if nl > base_n and nl <= MAX_LEN_LIMIT:
            cand_lens.append(nl)

    unique_lens = []
    for vl in cand_lens:
        vl = int(vl)
        if vl <= 50 or vl > MAX_LEN_LIMIT:
            continue
        dup = False
        for uu in unique_lens:
            if abs(vl - uu) <= 200:
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            unique_lens.append(vl)
    unique_lens.sort()

    for nl in unique_lens:
        rem = hard_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break
        if nl == len(best):
            continue
        cand = interpolate_sequence(best, nl)
        sc = current_score(cand)
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best, 6)
            print(f"Length scan improved n={nl} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    n_best = int(len(best))

    # Main medium-temperature optimization on current best length.
    rem = hard_end - time.monotonic()
    phase_budget = min(rem * 0.25, 280.0)
    if phase_budget > 20.0:
        print(f"Phase A: mirror n={n_best} budget={phase_budget:.0f}s", flush=True)
        cand, sc = mirror_smooth(
            best,
            phase_budget,
            beta_start=1.0,
            beta_end=25.0,
            stall_s=45.0,
            noise_amp=0.03,
            seed=1001,
            verbose=True,
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best, 6)
            print(f"Phase A improved score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    # Try slightly longer sequences from the best point.
    long_offsets = (1500, 3000, 5000)
    long_cands = []
    for off in long_offsets:
        nl = n_best + off
        if nl > n_best + 500 and nl <= MAX_LEN_LIMIT:
            long_cands.append(nl)
    long_cands = long_cands[:2]

    for nl in long_cands:
        rem = hard_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 80.0:
            break
        phase_budget = min(rem * 0.22, 260.0)
        if phase_budget < 35.0:
            break
        print(f"Phase B: mirror n={nl} budget={phase_budget:.0f}s", flush=True)
        cand0 = interpolate_sequence(best, nl)
        cand, sc = mirror_smooth(
            cand0,
            phase_budget,
            beta_start=1.0,
            beta_end=30.0,
            stall_s=45.0,
            noise_amp=0.035,
            seed=2002,
            verbose=False,
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best, 6)
            print(f"Phase B improved n={len(best)} score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    # Perturbed restart on current best length.
    rem = hard_end - time.monotonic()
    phase_budget = min(rem * 0.18, 240.0)
    if phase_budget > 30.0:
        n_best = int(len(best))
        base = random_smooth_seq(n_best, 42424)
        amp = 0.08
        cand0 = clip_normalize((1.0 - amp) * best + amp * base, 1e-10)
        print(f"Phase C: perturbed mirror n={n_best} budget={phase_budget:.0f}s", flush=True)
        cand, sc = mirror_smooth(
            cand0,
            phase_budget,
            beta_start=0.7,
            beta_end=40.0,
            stall_s=35.0,
            noise_amp=0.02,
            seed=3003,
            verbose=False,
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best, 6)
            print(f"Phase C improved score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    # Final high-temperature-active polishing.
    rem = hard_end - time.monotonic()
    phase_budget = min(rem * 0.55, 650.0)
    if phase_budget > 30.0:
        n_best = int(len(best))
        print(f"Phase D: final high-beta polish n={n_best} budget={phase_budget:.0f}s", flush=True)
        cand, sc = mirror_smooth(
            best,
            phase_budget,
            beta_start=10.0,
            beta_end=160.0,
            stall_s=30.0,
            noise_amp=0.012,
            seed=5005,
            verbose=True,
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            add_to_pool(pool, best_score, best, 6)
            print(f"Phase D improved score={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    # Final exact validation and conservative normalization.
    final_arr = clip_normalize(best, 1e-12)
    final_safe = safe_eval(final_arr)
    print(f"Final internal score={best_score:.7f}, safe eval={final_safe:.7f}", flush=True)

    # If there is spare time, check at most one alternative length from the pool.
    if (total_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_start)) > 25.0:
        evaluated_lens = [len(final_arr)]
        checked = 0
        for item in pool:
            if checked >= 1:
                break
            arr = clip_normalize(item[1], 1e-12)
            ln = len(arr)
            if ln in evaluated_lens:
                continue
            sc = safe_eval(arr)
            evaluated_lens.append(ln)
            checked += 1
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_safe:
                final_arr = arr
                final_safe = sc
                print(f"Alternative exact-improved length={ln} score={sc:.7f}", flush=True)

    final_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(final_arr), final_arr, 0.0)
    final_arr = np.maximum(final_arr, 0.0)
    s = float(final_arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-8:
        final_arr = np.ones(max(1, len(final_arr)))
    else:
        final_arr = final_arr / s

    return [float(x) for x in final_arr]
```
